# Root Bot Developer Documentation — API Reference

> TypeScript API reference for the Root Bot SDK. For conceptual guides, see llms-bot-guides.txt.
> Generated: 2026-04-20 | Content-Hash: cc495d347f9d

## Instructions

- Apps use `@rootsdk/server-app` (server) and `@rootsdk/client` (client). Bots use `@rootsdk/server-bot`.
- TypeScript-first. All examples use TypeScript.
- Server code runs in the Root cloud. Client code runs in a Chromium container.
- For real-time data, use event subscriptions, not polling.
- All async methods return Promises. Use async/await.
- Bots are server-only — same community API as apps, but no client UI.

> **Size:** ~151K tokens

## Table of Contents

### Bot API Reference

- [Bot API Reference](#file-bot-api-reference-index)

#### Access Rules

- [AccessRule](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrule) - Represents a permission override for a specific role or member on a specific channel or channel group.
- [AccessRuleBulkCreateEditDeleteRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrulebulkcreateeditdeleterequest) - Object type with properties: creates, deletes, edits (Access Rules).
- [AccessRuleClient](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessruleclient) - Service client for managing access rules that control permissions for specific roles or members on channels and channel groups.
- [AccessRuleCreateRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrulecreaterequest) - Request object for creating a new access rule that overrides permissions for a role or member on a channel or channel group.
- [AccessRuleCreateRoleOrMemberRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrulecreateroleormemberrequest) - Request object for creating an access rule for a specific role or member.
- [AccessRuleDeleteRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessruledeleterequest) - Request object for deleting an access rule.
- [AccessRuleEditRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessruleeditrequest) - Request object for modifying an existing access rule's permission overlay.
- [AccessRuleGetRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrulegetrequest) - Request object for retrieving a single access rule.
- [AccessRuleListByChannelOrChannelGroupRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrulelistbychannelorchannelgrouprequest) - Request object for listing all access rules that apply to a specific channel or channel group.
- [AccessRuleListByRoleOrMemberRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrulelistbyroleormemberrequest) - Request object for listing all access rules that apply to a specific role or member.
- [AccessRuleUpdateRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessruleupdaterequest) - Request object for batch creating, editing, and deleting access rules in a single operation.

#### App

- [AppGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-appguid) - Identifies an app or bot. Branded with `RootGuidType.App`. Both apps and bots share this type.

#### Assets

- [AssetAspectRatio](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-assetaspectratio) - The aspect ratio of an image or video asset, expressed as a horizontal-to-vertical ratio.
- [AssetFile](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-assetfile) - Metadata for an uploaded file asset that is not an image or video (e.g. a PDF, ZIP, or document).
- [AssetGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-assetguid) - Identifies an uploaded asset (image, video, or file). Branded with `RootGuidType.Asset`.
- [AssetImage](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-assetimage) - Metadata for an uploaded image asset. Contains links to the image at various resolutions, an inline WebP thumbnail, and aspect ratio information.
- [AssetImageLink](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-assetimagelink) - A URL to an image asset at a specific resolution. An `AssetImage` contains multiple links, one per available resolution.
- [AssetInformation](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-assetinformation) - Metadata for a single asset. Returned by `AssetClient.get()` and included in message events via `MessageReferenceMaps`.
- [AssetPreview](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-assetpreview) - Link preview metadata for an asset. Generated automatically by the platform when a URL or media file is processed.
- [AssetPreviewAudio](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-assetpreviewaudio) - Audio-specific preview metadata within an `AssetPreview`.
- [AssetPreviewImage](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-assetpreviewimage) - A thumbnail image within an `AssetPreview`.
- [AssetPreviewVideo](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-assetpreviewvideo) - Video-specific preview metadata within an `AssetPreview`.
- [AssetPreviewWebpage](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-assetpreviewwebpage) - Webpage-specific preview metadata within an `AssetPreview`. Generated when the platform processes a URL that points to a web page.
- [AssetVideo](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-assetvideo) - Metadata for an uploaded video asset. Provides streaming URLs (HLS and DASH), a thumbnail, an animated preview, and a direct download URL.

#### Billing

- [BillingPaymentType](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-billingpaymenttype) - Enumeration (Billing).
- [BillingSubscriptionType](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-billingsubscriptiontype) - Enumeration (Billing).

#### Bot Lifecycle

- [BotStartingCallback](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-botstartingcallback) - Callback invoked during bot server startup, after the database initializes but before the server begins processing events.
- [BotStoppingCallback](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-botstoppingcallback) - Callback invoked during graceful bot server shutdown, before the server disconnects from Root's infrastructure.

#### Channels

- [ChannelDirectoryEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-channeldirectoryevent) - Enum providing string constants for directory event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `ChannelDirectoryClient`.
- [ChannelEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-channelevent) - Enum providing string constants for channel event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `ChannelClient`.
- [ChannelFileEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-channelfileevent) - Enum providing string constants for file event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `ChannelFileClient`.
- [ChannelGroupEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-channelgroupevent) - Enum of event names for channel group changes. Use these values when subscribing to events on `ChannelGroupClient`.
- [ChannelMessageEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-channelmessageevent) - Enum providing string constants for message event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `ChannelMessageClient`.
- [ChannelType](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-channeltype) - Enum defining the types of channels that can be created in a community.
- [ChannelWebRtcEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-channelwebrtcevent) - Enum providing string constants for voice channel event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `ChannelWebRtcClient`.
- [Channel](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channel) - Represents a channel within a community. Channels are the primary containers for content and belong to channel groups.
- [ChannelClient](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelclient) - Service client for managing channels within a community. Channels are the primary containers for content within channel groups, supporting different...
- [ChannelCreatedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelcreatedevent) - Event data emitted when a channel becomes visible to your code.
- [ChannelCreatedHandler](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelcreatedhandler) - Callback invoked when a channel becomes visible to your code as a side effect of an API operation.
- [ChannelCreateRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelcreaterequest) - Request object for creating a new channel within a channel group.
- [ChannelDeletedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldeletedevent) - Event data emitted when a channel is no longer visible to your code.
- [ChannelDeletedHandler](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldeletedhandler) - Callback invoked when a channel is no longer visible to your code as a side effect of an API operation.
- [ChannelDeleteRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldeleterequest) - Request object for deleting a channel.
- [ChannelDirectory](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldirectory) - Represents a directory (folder) within a channel's file system.
- [ChannelDirectoryClient](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldirectoryclient) - Service client for managing directories (folders) within a channel's file system.
- [ChannelDirectoryCreatedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldirectorycreatedevent) - Event payload emitted when a directory is created.
- [ChannelDirectoryCreateRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldirectorycreaterequest) - Request object for creating a new directory in a channel.
- [ChannelDirectoryDeletedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldirectorydeletedevent) - Event payload emitted when a directory is deleted.
- [ChannelDirectoryDeleteRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldirectorydeleterequest) - Request object for deleting a directory and all its contents.
- [ChannelDirectoryEditedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldirectoryeditedevent) - Event payload emitted when a directory is renamed.
- [ChannelDirectoryEditRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldirectoryeditrequest) - Request object for renaming a directory.
- [ChannelDirectoryEditResponse](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldirectoryeditresponse) - Response object returned after renaming a directory.
- [ChannelDirectoryEvents](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldirectoryevents) - Event map type for `ChannelDirectoryClient`. This type defines the event signatures for directory-related events.
- [ChannelDirectoryGetRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldirectorygetrequest) - Request object for retrieving a single directory.
- [ChannelDirectoryListRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldirectorylistrequest) - Request object for listing all directories in a channel. Returns a flat array containing all directories regardless of nesting depth.
- [ChannelDirectoryMovedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldirectorymovedevent) - Event payload emitted when a directory is moved to a different parent.
- [ChannelDirectoryMoveRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldirectorymoverequest) - Request object for moving a directory to a different parent directory.
- [ChannelDirectoryMoveResponse](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldirectorymoveresponse) - Response object returned after moving a directory.
- [ChannelEditedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeleditedevent) - Event data emitted when a visible channel's properties or permissions change.
- [ChannelEditedHandler](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeleditedhandler) - Callback invoked when a visible channel's properties or permissions change as a side effect of an API operation.
- [ChannelEditRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeleditrequest) - Request object for editing an existing channel's properties.
- [ChannelEvents](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelevents) - Event map type for `ChannelClient`. This type defines the event signatures for channel-related events.
- [ChannelFile](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfile) - Represents a file stored within a channel directory. Files contain metadata about uploaded content and link to assets in the asset system.
- [ChannelFileClient](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfileclient) - Service client for managing files within channel directories. Files are stored in directories and linked to assets in the asset system.
- [ChannelFileCreatedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfilecreatedevent) - Event payload emitted when a file is created.
- [ChannelFileCreateRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfilecreaterequest) - Request object for creating a new file entry in a directory.
- [ChannelFileDeletedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfiledeletedevent) - Event payload emitted when a file is deleted.
- [ChannelFileDeleteRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfiledeleterequest) - Request object for deleting a file.
- [ChannelFileEditedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfileeditedevent) - Event payload emitted when a file is renamed.
- [ChannelFileEditRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfileeditrequest) - Request object for renaming a file.
- [ChannelFileEditResponse](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfileeditresponse) - Response object returned after renaming a file.
- [ChannelFileEvents](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfileevents) - Event map type for `ChannelFileClient`. This type defines the event signatures for file-related events.
- [ChannelFileGetRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfilegetrequest) - Request object for retrieving a single file.
- [ChannelFileListRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfilelistrequest) - Request object for listing all files in a directory.
- [ChannelFileMovedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfilemovedevent) - Event payload emitted when a file is moved to a different directory.
- [ChannelFileMoveRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfilemoverequest) - Request object for moving a file to a different directory.
- [ChannelFileMoveResponse](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfilemoveresponse) - Response object returned after moving a file.
- [ChannelFileSearchCommunityRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfilesearchcommunityrequest) - Request object for searching files by name across multiple channels.

- [ChannelFileSearchResult](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelfilesearchresult) - Search result for a single channel in a community-wide file search.
- [ChannelGetRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgetrequest) - Request object for retrieving a single channel by its ID.
- [ChannelGroup](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroup) - Represents a container that organizes channels within a community.
- [ChannelGroupClient](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupclient) - Service client for managing channel groups within a community.
- [ChannelGroupCreatedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupcreatedevent) - Event payload emitted when a channel group becomes visible to your code.
- [ChannelGroupCreatedHandler](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupcreatedhandler) - Callback invoked when a channel group becomes visible to your code as a side effect of an API operation.
- [ChannelGroupCreateRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupcreaterequest) - Request to create a new channel group in the community.

- [ChannelGroupDeletedHandler](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupdeletedhandler) - Callback invoked when a channel group is no longer visible to your code as a side effect of an API operation.
- [ChannelGroupDeleteRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupdeleterequest) - Request to delete a channel group. Deleting a channel group also deletes all channels within it.
- [ChannelGroupEditedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupeditedevent) - Event payload emitted when a visible channel group's properties or permissions change.
- [ChannelGroupEditedHandler](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupeditedhandler) - Callback invoked when a visible channel group's properties or permissions change as a side effect of an API operation.
- [ChannelGroupEditRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupeditrequest) - Request to edit an existing channel group.
- [ChannelGroupEvents](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupevents) - Event map type for `ChannelGroupClient`. This type defines the event signatures for channel group events.
- [ChannelGroupGetRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupgetrequest) - Request to retrieve a single channel group.
- [ChannelGroupGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupguid) - Identifies a channel group within a community. Branded with `RootGuidType.ChannelGroup`.
- [ChannelGroupMovedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupmovedevent) - Event payload emitted when a visible channel group is moved to a different position in the sidebar.
- [ChannelGroupMoveRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupmoverequest) - Request to move a channel group to a different position in the sidebar.
- [ChannelGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelguid) - Identifies a channel within a community. Branded with `RootGuidType.Channel`.
- [ChannelListRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channellistrequest) - Request object for listing all channels within a channel group.
- [ChannelMessage](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelmessage) - Represents a message in a channel.
- [ChannelMessageClient](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelmessageclient) - Service client for managing messages within channels. Messages are the primary content units in text channels, supporting rich content with...

- [ChannelMessagePinDeleteRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelmessagepindeleterequest) - Request object for removing a pin from a message.

- [ChannelMessagePinListResponse](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelmessagepinlistresponse) - Response object returned when listing pinned messages in a channel.

- [ChannelMessageReactionDeletedFullEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelmessagereactiondeletedfullevent) - Event payload emitted when all reactions of a given shortcode are removed from a message, regardless of which users added them.

- [ChannelMessageSetViewTimeRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelmessagesetviewtimerequest) - Request object for updating the last viewed timestamp in a channel.

- [ChannelMoveRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelmoverequest) - Request object for moving a channel to a different position or channel group.

- [ChannelPermission](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelpermission) - Permissions that control actions on a specific channel or its contents.
- [ChannelWebRtc](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelwebrtc) - Object type with properties: channelId, communityId, deviceId, isAdminDeafened, ... (Channels).
- [ChannelWebRtcClient](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelwebrtcclient) - Service client for monitoring and moderating voice channel participants.
- [ChannelWebRtcEvents](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelwebrtcevents) - Event map type for `ChannelWebRtcClient`. This type defines the event signatures for voice channel-related events.
- [ChannelWebRtcKickRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelwebrtckickrequest) - Request object for removing a participant from a voice channel.
- [ChannelWebRtcListRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelwebrtclistrequest) - Request object for listing participants in a voice channel.
- [ChannelWebRtcListResponse](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelwebrtclistresponse) - Response object returned when listing voice channel participants.
- [ChannelWebRtcSetMuteAndDeafenOtherRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelwebrtcsetmuteanddeafenotherrequest) - Request object for muting or deafening another user in a voice channel.
- [ChannelWebRtcUserAttachEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelwebrtcuserattachevent) - Event payload emitted when a user joins a voice channel.
- [ChannelWebRtcUserDetachEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelwebrtcuserdetachevent) - Event payload emitted when a user leaves a voice channel.
- [ChannelWebRtcUserDeviceSetDataChannelEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelwebrtcuserdevicesetdatachannelevent) - Event payload emitted when a user's data channel configuration changes in a voice channel.
- [ChannelWebRtcUserDeviceSetStatusEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelwebrtcuserdevicesetstatusevent) - Event payload emitted when a user's mute or deafen state changes in a voice channel.
- [ChannelWebRtcUserDeviceSetTransportEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelwebrtcuserdevicesettransportevent) - Event payload emitted when a user enables or disables their microphone, camera, or screen sharing in a voice channel.

#### Community

- [CommunityAppLogType](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-communityapplogtype) - Enum defining severity levels for community app log entries.
- [CommunityEmojiEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-communityemojievent) - Enum providing string constants for community emoji event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `CommunityEmojiClient`.
- [CommunityEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-communityevent) - Enum providing string constants for community event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `CommunityClient`.
- [CommunityLeaveReason](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-communityleavereason) - Enum indicating why a user left a community.
- [CommunityLogAction](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-communitylogaction) - Enumeration (Community).
- [CommunityMemberBanEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-communitymemberbanevent) - Enum providing string constants for community member ban event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `CommunityMemberBanClient`.
- [CommunityMemberEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-communitymemberevent) - Enum providing string constants for community member event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `CommunityMemberClient`.
- [CommunityMemberRoleEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-communitymemberroleevent) - Enum providing string constants for member role event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `CommunityMemberRoleClient`.
- [CommunityRoleEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-communityroleevent) - Enum providing string constants for role event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `CommunityRoleClient`.
- [Community](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-community) - Represents a Root community and its properties.
- [CommunityAppGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityappguid) - Identifies an app or bot installed in a specific community. Branded with `RootGuidType.CommunityApp`. Both apps and bots share this type.
- [CommunityAppLog](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityapplog) - A log entry written by an app to a community's diagnostic log.
- [CommunityAppLogClient](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityapplogclient) - Client for writing diagnostic logs visible to community members with the **Manage Apps** permission.
- [CommunityAppLogCreateRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityapplogcreaterequest) - Request object for writing a log entry to the community's app log.
- [CommunityAppLogCreateResponse](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityapplogcreateresponse) - Response object returned when creating a community app log entry.
- [CommunityClient](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityclient) - Service client for managing community settings and properties.
- [CommunityEditedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityeditedevent) - Event data emitted when a community's properties are modified.
- [CommunityEditRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityeditrequest) - Request object for editing a community's properties.
- [CommunityEmoji](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityemoji) - Represents a custom emoji in a community. Community emojis are custom images that members can reference by shortcode in messages.
- [CommunityEmojiClient](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityemojiclient) - Service client for managing custom emojis within a community. Community emojis are custom images associated with shortcodes that members can use in...
- [CommunityEmojiCreatedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityemojicreatedevent) - Event payload emitted when a custom emoji is added to the community.
- [CommunityEmojiDeletedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityemojideletedevent) - Event payload emitted when a custom emoji is removed from the community.
- [CommunityEmojiDeleteRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityemojideleterequest) - Request object for deleting a community emoji.
- [CommunityEmojiEvents](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityemojievents) - Event map type for `CommunityEmojiClient`. This type defines the event signatures for community emoji-related events.
- [CommunityEmojiGetRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityemojigetrequest) - Request object for retrieving a single community emoji.
- [CommunityEvents](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityevents) - Event map type for `CommunityClient`. This type defines the event signatures for community-related events.
- [CommunityGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityguid) - Identifies a community. Branded with `RootGuidType.Community`.
- [CommunityJoinedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityjoinedevent) - Event payload emitted when a user joins the community.
- [CommunityJoinThrottle](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityjointhrottle) - Configuration for rate-limiting new member joins to a community. Used to prevent rapid influxes of new members that could overwhelm moderation.
- [CommunityLeaveEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityleaveevent) - Event payload emitted when a user leaves the community, whether voluntarily, by being kicked, or by being banned.
- [CommunityMember](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymember) - Represents a user's membership in a community, including their profile information, assigned roles, and membership dates.
- [CommunityMemberAttachEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberattachevent) - Event payload emitted every time a community member opens the community on any device.
- [CommunityMemberBan](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberban) - Represents a ban record for a community member. Bans prevent users from rejoining the community until the ban is deleted or expires.
- [CommunityMemberBanBulk](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberbanbulk) - Response object for bulk ban operations, indicating which users were successfully banned.
- [CommunityMemberBanClient](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberbanclient) - Service client for managing member bans and kicks within a community.
- [CommunityMemberBanCreateBulkRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberbancreatebulkrequest) - Request object for banning multiple members from the community in a single operation.
- [CommunityMemberBanCreatedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberbancreatedevent) - Event payload emitted when a user is banned from the community.
- [CommunityMemberBanCreateRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberbancreaterequest) - Request object for banning a single member from the community.
- [CommunityMemberBanDeletedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberbandeletedevent) - Event payload emitted when a ban is revoked, allowing the user to rejoin the community.
- [CommunityMemberBanDeleteRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberbandeleterequest) - Request object for removing a ban from a user.
- [CommunityMemberBanEvents](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberbanevents) - Event map type for `CommunityMemberBanClient`. This type defines the event signatures for community member ban-related events.
- [CommunityMemberBanGetRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberbangetrequest) - Request object for retrieving a specific ban by user ID.
- [CommunityMemberBanGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberbanguid) - Identifies a member ban record within a community. Branded with `RootGuidType.CommunityMemberBan`.
- [CommunityMemberBanKickBulkRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberbankickbulkrequest) - Request object for kicking multiple members from the community in a single operation.
- [CommunityMemberBanKickBulkResponse](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberbankickbulkresponse) - Response object for bulk kick operations, indicating which users were successfully kicked.
- [CommunityMemberBanKickRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberbankickrequest) - Request object for kicking a single member from the community. Kicking removes a member without creating a ban record, allowing them to rejoin.
- [CommunityMemberClient](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberclient) - Service client for retrieving information about members within a community.
- [CommunityMemberDetachEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberdetachevent) - Event payload emitted every time a community member closes the community on any device.
- [CommunityMemberEditedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymembereditedevent) - Event payload emitted when a community member's nickname changes.
- [CommunityMemberEvents](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberevents) - Event map type for `CommunityMemberClient`. This type defines the event signatures for member-related events.
- [CommunityMemberGetRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymembergetrequest) - Request object for retrieving a single community member by user ID.
- [CommunityMemberInvite](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberinvite) - Represents a pending invitation for a user to join a community.
- [CommunityMemberInviteClient](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberinviteclient) - Service client for managing member invitations within a community.
- [CommunityMemberInviteDeleteRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberinvitedeleterequest) - Request object for revoking a pending member invitation.
- [CommunityMemberInviteGetRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberinvitegetrequest) - Request object for retrieving a specific member invitation.
- [CommunityMemberInviteGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberinviteguid) - Identifies a community invite. Branded with `RootGuidType.CommunityMemberInvite`.
- [CommunityMemberListRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberlistrequest) - Request object for retrieving multiple community members by their user IDs.
- [CommunityMemberRoleAddRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberroleaddrequest) - Request object for assigning a role to one or more community members.
- [CommunityMemberRoleClient](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberroleclient) - Service client for managing role assignments on community members.
- [CommunityMemberRoleCreatedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberrolecreatedevent) - Event payload emitted when a role is assigned to one or more community members.
- [CommunityMemberRoleDeletedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberroledeletedevent) - Event payload emitted when a role is removed from one or more community members.
- [CommunityMemberRoleEvents](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberroleevents) - Event map type for `CommunityMemberRoleClient`. This type defines the event signatures for member role assignment events.
- [CommunityMemberRoleListRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberrolelistrequest) - Request object for listing the roles assigned to a community member.
- [CommunityMemberRoleListResponse](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberrolelistresponse) - Response object containing the roles assigned to a community member.
- [CommunityMemberRoleRemoveRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberroleremoverequest) - Request object for removing a role from one or more community members.
- [CommunityMemberRoleSetPrimaryEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberrolesetprimaryevent) - Event payload emitted when a member's primary role is changed. The primary role determines the color of the member's name in the UI.
- [CommunityMemberRoleSetPrimaryRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitymemberrolesetprimaryrequest) - Request object for setting a member's primary displayed role.
- [CommunityPermission](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitypermission) - Permissions that control access to community-level entities and settings.
- [CommunityPermissionEditedHandler](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitypermissioneditedhandler) - Callback invoked when community-wide permissions change as a side effect of an API operation. Receives the updated `CommunityPermission`.
- [CommunityPermissionUpdateEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitypermissionupdateevent) - Object type with properties: channelGroupsCreated, channelGroupsDeleted, channelGroupsEdited, channelGroupsMoved, ... (Community).
- [CommunityRole](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityrole) - Represents a role within a community. Roles define permission sets that can be assigned to members.
- [CommunityRoleClient](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityroleclient) - Service client for managing roles within a community. Roles define sets of permissions that can be assigned to community members, controlling what...
- [CommunityRoleCreatedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityrolecreatedevent) - Event payload emitted when a new role is created in the community.
- [CommunityRoleCreateRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityrolecreaterequest) - Request object for creating a new role in the community.
- [CommunityRoleDeletedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityroledeletedevent) - Event payload emitted when a role is deleted from the community.
- [CommunityRoleDeleteRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityroledeleterequest) - Request object for deleting a role from the community.
- [CommunityRoleEditedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityroleeditedevent) - Event payload emitted when a role's properties are modified.
- [CommunityRoleEditRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityroleeditrequest) - Request object for modifying an existing role's properties.
- [CommunityRoleEvents](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityroleevents) - Event map type for `CommunityRoleClient`. This type defines the event signatures for role-related events.
- [CommunityRoleGetRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityrolegetrequest) - Request object for retrieving a single role by its ID.
- [CommunityRoleGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityroleguid) - Identifies a role within a community. Branded with `RootGuidType.CommunityRole`.
- [CommunityRoleMovedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityrolemovedevent) - Event payload emitted when a role's display order changes in the community's role list.
- [CommunityRoleMoveRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communityrolemoverequest) - Request object for changing a role's display position.

#### Content Moderation

#### Global Settings

- [GlobalSettingsEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-globalsettingsevent) - Enumeration (Global Settings).
- [GlobalSetting](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-globalsetting) - Union type representing any individual setting value. The actual type depends on the setting's type key declared in the manifest.
- [GlobalSettingButtonEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-globalsettingbuttonevent) - Event payload delivered when a community member presses a `button` setting.
- [GlobalSettingChannel](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-globalsettingchannel) - The value of a `channel` setting: `ChannelGuid[]`. Returns an array of channel IDs selected by the community admin.
- [GlobalSettingChannelGroup](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-globalsettingchannelgroup) - The value of a `channelGroup` setting: `ChannelGroupGuid[]`. Returns an array of channel group IDs selected by the community admin.
- [GlobalSettingCheckbox](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-globalsettingcheckbox) - The value of a `checkbox` setting: `boolean`. Returns `true` or `false` based on the community admin's selection.
- [GlobalSettingColor](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-globalsettingcolor) - The value of a `color` setting: `string | undefined`. Returns the hex color code selected by the community admin (e.g., `#ff0000`), or `undefined` if...
- [GlobalSettingDate](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-globalsettingdate) - The value of a `date` setting: `{ year: number, month: number, day: number }`. Returns the date selected by the community admin.
- [GlobalSettingNumber](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-globalsettingnumber) - The value of a `number` setting: `number | undefined`. Returns the numeric value entered by the community admin, or `undefined` if no value has been...
- [GlobalSettingRoleOrMember](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-globalsettingroleormember) - A `ReadOnlyMemberGroup` representing the roles and members selected by the community admin.
- [GlobalSettings](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-globalsettings) - Community-scoped global settings container. Provides keyed access to setting values by group and item, and emits events when settings change.
- [GlobalSettingSelect](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-globalsettingselect) - The value of a `select` setting: `string[]`. Returns an array of selected option values from the dropdown defined in the manifest.
- [GlobalSettingsEvents](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-globalsettingsevents) - Event map type for `GlobalSettings`. This type defines the event signatures for global settings events.
- [GlobalSettingsUpdateEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-globalsettingsupdateevent) - Event payload delivered when a community member changes any global setting value.
- [GlobalSettingText](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-globalsettingtext) - The value of a `text` setting: `string | undefined`. Returns the text entered by the community admin, or `undefined` if no value has been set.
- [GlobalSettingTime](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-globalsettingtime) - The value of a `time` setting: `{ hours: number, minutes: number, seconds: number }`. Returns the time-of-day selected by the community admin.
- [GlobalSettingTimestamp](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-globalsettingtimestamp) - The value of a `timestamp` setting: `Date | undefined`. Returns a JavaScript `Date` object representing the timestamp selected by the community...

#### Job Scheduler

- [JobInterval](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-jobinterval) - An enumeration that specifies the recurrence of a job in the job scheduler.
- [JobScheduleEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-jobscheduleevent) - Enum providing string constants for job notification event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `JobScheduler`.
- [JobCreateRequest](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-jobcreaterequest) - Encapsulates the specification of a job to be scheduled with the job scheduler.
- [JobData](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-jobdata) - Event payload emitted when a scheduled job fires. Received by handlers subscribed to `JobScheduleEvent.Job` or `JobScheduleEvent.JobMissed` events.
- [JobRecord](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-jobrecord) - Represents a scheduled job stored in the `JobScheduler`. This type is returned by `JobScheduler.create()`, `JobScheduler.get()`, and the various list...
- [JobSchedule](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-jobschedule) - Encapsulates the timing of a job to be scheduled with the job scheduler.
- [JobScheduleEvents](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-jobscheduleevents) - Event map type for `JobScheduler`. This type defines the event signatures for job notification events.
- [JobScheduler](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-jobscheduler) - Represents a job scheduling system that notifies you when it's time for one of your tasks to run.

#### Key-Value Store

- [KeyValue](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-keyvalue) - Represents a key-value pair returned by `KeyValueStore.select()`. Contains the key, value, and optional expiration date.
- [KeyValueStore](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-keyvaluestore) - `KeyValueStore` is a key-value storage mechanism. It provides an asynchronous API for storing, retrieving, updating,

#### Member Groups

- [MemberGroupEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-membergroupevent) - Enumeration (Member Groups).
- [MemberGroup](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-membergroup) - A named collection of individual users and community roles combined into a single audience.
- [MemberGroupClient](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-membergroupclient) - Type alias for `MemberGroupClientBase` (Member Groups).
- [MemberGroupEmptiedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-membergroupemptiedevent) - Payload for the `MemberGroupServiceEvent.UserGroupEmptied` event, emitted when a member group's resolved membership becomes empty.
- [MemberGroupEvents](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-membergroupevents) - Object type with properties: members, members, state, userGroup (Member Groups).
- [MemberGroupMembersAddedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-membergroupmembersaddedevent) - Payload for the `MemberGroupServiceEvent.MembersAdded` event, emitted when users are added to a member group.
- [MemberGroupMembersRemovedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-membergroupmembersremovedevent) - Payload for the `MemberGroupServiceEvent.MembersRemoved` event, emitted when users are removed from a member group.
- [MemberGroupShort](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-membergroupshort) - Lightweight member group metadata returned by `MemberGroupService.list()`. Contains identifying information but no membership data.
- [MemberGroupStateChangedEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-membergroupstatechangedevent) - Payload for the `MemberGroupServiceEvent.StateChanged` event, emitted when a member group's community role list changes.

#### Messages

- [MessageDirectionTake](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-messagedirectiontake) - Controls the direction of message pagination when listing messages with `ChannelMessageClient.list()`.
- [MessagePayloadAction](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-messagepayloadaction) - Enumeration (Messages).
- [MessageType](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-messagetype) - Enum indicating the type of message.
- [MessageVoteType](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-messagevotetype) - Enumeration (Messages).
- [MessageGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-messageguid) - Identifies a message within a channel. Branded with `RootGuidType.Message`.
- [MessageReaction](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-messagereaction) - Represents a reaction on a message, used within `ChannelMessage` objects.
- [MessageReferenceMapChannel](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-messagereferencemapchannel) - A resolved channel mention containing the channel ID and its current name.
- [MessageReferenceMapCommunityRole](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-messagereferencemapcommunityrole) - A resolved role mention containing the role ID and its current name.
- [MessageReferenceMaps](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-messagereferencemaps) - Contains resolved display names for all references in a message, including user mentions, channel mentions, role mentions, and file attachments.
- [MessageReferenceMapUser](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-messagereferencemapuser) - A resolved user mention containing the user ID and their current display name.
- [MessageUri](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-messageuri) - A URI reference extracted from message content. URIs can reference users, roles, channels, assets, or external URLs.
- [MessageUriAttachment](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-messageuriattachment) - Metadata for a file attachment referenced in a message.

#### Root Core

- [RootApiException](#file-bot-api-reference-classes-rootapiexception) - Represents an error generated by the Root API. This error includes additional information such as error codes, payloads, and metadata.
- [RootGuidConverter](#file-bot-api-reference-classes-rootguidconverter) - Static utility class that provides a subset of `RootGuidUtils` methods. Delegates to `RootGuidUtils` internally.
- [RootGuidUtils](#file-bot-api-reference-classes-rootguidutils) - Static utility class for converting and inspecting Root GUIDs. Cannot be instantiated.
- [RootGuidType](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-rootguidtype) - Enum identifying the entity type encoded in a Root GUID. Used to brand GUID types at compile time and to inspect GUIDs at runtime via...
- [RootBotLifecycle](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-rootbotlifecycle) - Manages the startup and shutdown sequence for your bot's server. Access via `rootServer.lifecycle`.
- [RootBotStartState](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-rootbotstartstate) - Snapshot of the community's current state, passed to your `BotStartingCallback` during bot server startup.
- [RootDatabaseConfig](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-rootdatabaseconfig) - Root-provided configuration for database access. Root automatically creates an instance of `RootDatabaseConfig` for you and exposes it via the...
- [RootGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-rootguid) - The base type for all Root identifiers. A branded string that carries a `RootGuidType` at compile time.
- [RootServer](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-rootserver) - This type is your entry point to most of the Root server-side APIs. Root automatically creates an instance of this type and provides it for your use.
- [RootServerService](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-rootserverservice) - A service definition object that you register with `RootAppLifecycle.addService()` to enable custom client-to-server communication.

#### Users

- [UserCommunityInviteConnection](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-usercommunityinviteconnection) - Enumeration (Users).
- [UserDeviceOnlineStatus](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-userdeviceonlinestatus) - Enumeration (Users).
- [UserDirectMessageInviteConnection](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-userdirectmessageinviteconnection) - Enumeration (Users).
- [UserFlagProperty](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-userflagproperty) - Enumeration (Users).
- [UserFriendshipInviteConnection](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-userfriendshipinviteconnection) - Enumeration (Users).
- [UserOnlineStatus](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-useronlinestatus) - A community member's current online status.
- [UserType](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-usertype) - Enumeration (Users).
- [UserGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-userguid) - Identifies a user, app, or bot account. Branded with `RootGuidType.Person` or `RootGuidType.App`, since users, apps, and bots can all author messages...
- [UserSetProfileEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-usersetprofileevent) - Event payload emitted when a user updates any part of their profile (picture, description, banner, or status).

#### Voice & WebRTC

- [WebRtcUserDeviceEvent](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-webrtcuserdeviceevent) - Represents a user's device state in a WebRTC voice or video channel.
- [WebRtcUserInfoResponse](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-webrtcuserinforesponse) - Information about a participant in a voice channel session.

#### Other

- [CustomMemberGroupGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-custommembergroupguid) - Identifies a custom member group within a community. Branded with `RootGuidType.CustomMemberGroup`.
- [EmojiGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-emojiguid) - Identifies a custom emoji within a community. Branded with `RootGuidType.Emoji`.
- [ParentMessage](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-parentmessage) - Represents a message that another message is replying to.
- [ReadOnlyMemberGroup](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-readonlymembergroup) - Read-only view of a member group. Contains all the same properties as `MemberGroup` but without mutation methods.
- [RoleOrMemberGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-roleormemberguid) - Identifies either a role, user, app, or bot. Branded with `RootGuidType.CommunityRole`, `RootGuidType.Person`, or `RootGuidType.App`.
- [TypedEventEmitter](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-typedeventemitter) - A type-safe wrapper around the standard Node.js `EventEmitter` interface.
- [UnknownGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-unknownguid) - GUID string for identifying unknown entities.
- [EmptyGuid](#file-bot-api-reference-variables-emptyguid) - The zero-value GUID. Use this instead of an empty string when a `RootGuid` is required but no entity exists.
- [rootServer](#file-bot-api-reference-variables-rootserver) - The singleton entry point for the server-side SDK. Provides access to the community API, data store, lifecycle, job scheduler, member groups, and...
- [WellKnownRootGuids](#file-bot-api-reference-variables-wellknownrootguids) - Built-in GUIDs for system entities. `CommunityRoles.EveryoneRole` is the default role assigned to every community member.

# Bot API Reference

TypeScript API reference for the Root Bot SDK. Covers community access, persistence, job scheduling, messaging, moderation, and server lifecycle hooks.

---

## File: bot-api-reference/classes/RootApiException.md

Represents an error generated by the Root API. This error includes additional information such as error codes, payloads, and metadata.

## Extends

- `Error`

## Properties

### errorCode

> **errorCode**: [`ErrorCodeType`](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-errorcodetype) = `ErrorCodeType.Unspecified`

The specific error code related to the error.

### id?

> `optional` **id**: [`RootGuid`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-rootguid)

Optional unique identifier for this error instance.

### parentId

> **parentId**: [`RootGuid`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-rootguid) | `undefined`

Optional identifier for the parent error, if this error is part of a chain.

### payload?

> `optional` **payload**: `RootGrpcExceptionPayload`

The specific payload associated with this error, which can be one of several predefined exception payload types.

### whatId

> **whatId**: [`RootGuid`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-rootguid) | `undefined`

Identifier for what caused the error.

### whereId

> **whereId**: [`RootGuid`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-rootguid) | `undefined`

Identifier for where the error occurred.

### whoId?

> `optional` **whoId**: [`UserGuid`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-userguid)

Optional identifier for the entity responsible for the error.

## File: bot-api-reference/classes/RootGuidUtils.md

Static utility class for converting and inspecting Root GUIDs. Cannot be instantiated.

## Methods

### stringToUint8Array()

> `static` **stringToUint8Array**(`uuid`: `string`): `Uint8Array`

Parses a GUID string (base64 or standard UUID format) into a byte array.

#### Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `uuid` | `string` | A GUID string in either format. |

#### Returns

`Uint8Array`

A `Uint8Array` of 16 bytes.

### toMilliseconds()

> `static` **toMilliseconds**(`strData`: `string`): `number`

Extracts the creation timestamp from a GUID as milliseconds since an internal epoch. Use the returned value to compare the relative age of GUIDs or sort entities by creation time.

#### Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `strData` | `string` | A GUID string. |

#### Returns

`number`

Milliseconds since the Root epoch.

### toRootGuidType()

> `static` **toRootGuidType**(`strData`: `string`): [`RootGuidType`](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-rootguidtype)

Extracts the `RootGuidType` from a GUID string.

#### Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `strData` | `string` | A GUID string. |

#### Returns

[`RootGuidType`](#file-bot-api-reference-enumerations-rootguidtype)

The `RootGuidType` of the entity.

### toString()

> `static` **toString**(`data`: `RootUuid` | `undefined`): `string`

Converts internal UUID data to a base64-encoded GUID string.

#### Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `data` | `RootUuid` | `undefined` | Internal UUID data. |

#### Returns

`string`

A base64-encoded GUID string.

### toStringFromArray()

| ------ | ------ |
| `data` | `Uint8Array` |

### toUuidString()

| ------ |
| `T` *extends* `string` |

## File: bot-api-reference/enumerations/CommunityMemberEvent.md

Enum providing string constants for community member event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `CommunityMemberClient`.

## Enumeration Members

### CommunityMemberAttach

> **CommunityMemberAttach**: `"communityMember.attach"`

Emitted when a community member becomes present in the community on a device. This happens when:

- **Desktop client:** The user opens the community in a tab (including popout windows). All open tabs are attached simultaneously — switching between tabs does not trigger additional attach/detach events.
- **Mobile client:** The user opens the community (only one community is open at a time).
- **Apps:** The app's host process connects to the community during deployment or dynamic assignment.
- **Reconnection:** When a client reconnects after a connection loss, it re-attaches to all previously open communities.

The event fires each time a device attaches, even if the user is already attached on another device.

### CommunityMemberDetach

> **CommunityMemberDetach**: `"communityMember.detach"`

Emitted when a community member is no longer present in the community on any device. This happens when:

- **Desktop client:** The user closes the community's tab or popout window. Switching away from a tab does not trigger detach.
- **Mobile client:** The user navigates away from the community.
- **Connection loss:** The user's connection drops and they have no remaining devices attached to the community.
- **Leaving or removal:** The user leaves the community, or is kicked/banned.
- **Apps:** The app's host process disconnects or the community is unassigned.

The event is only broadcast once the user has no remaining attached devices in the community — if they have the same community open on two devices and close one, no detach is emitted.

### UserSetProfile

> **UserSetProfile**: `"user.set.profile"`

Emitted when a user updates their username or profile picture.

---

## File: bot-api-reference/enumerations/JobScheduleEvent.md

Enum providing string constants for job notification event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `JobScheduler`.

## Enumeration Members

### Job

> **Job**: `"job"`

Raised at a job's regularly-scheduled time.

#### Example

```ts
import
{
  jobScheduler,
  JobData,
  JobScheduleEvent,
}
from '@rootsdk/server-app'

function subscribe()
{
  jobScheduler.addListener(JobScheduleEvent.Job, handleJobEvent);
}

const handleJobEvent = (event: JobData) =>
{
  const jobScheduleId: string = event.jobScheduleId;
  const resourceId:    string = event.resourceId;
  const tag:           string = event.tag || 'No tag';
  const jobTime:       Date   = event.jobTime;

  //...
};
```

### JobMissed

> **JobMissed**: `"job.missed"`

Raised if your server wasn't available at the regularly-scheduled event time.

#### Example

```ts
import
{
  jobScheduler,
  JobData,
  JobScheduleEvent,
}
from '@rootsdk/server-app'

function subscribe()
{
  jobScheduler.addListener(JobScheduleEvent.JobMissed, handleJobMissedEvent);
}

const handleJobMissedEvent = (event: JobData) =>
{
  const jobScheduleId: string = event.jobScheduleId;
  const resourceId:    string = event.resourceId;
  const tag:           string = event.tag || 'No tag';
  const jobTime:       Date   = event.jobTime;

  // ...
};
```

## File: bot-api-reference/enumerations/MessageDirectionTake.md

Controls the direction of message pagination when listing messages with `ChannelMessageClient.list()`. Used together with `dateAt` (a reference timestamp) and `limit` (maximum number of messages to return) on `ChannelMessageListRequest`.

## Enumeration Members

### Both

> **Both**: `3`

Return messages in both directions from `dateAt`, splitting the limit between newer and older results. Use when jumping to a specific point in message history and displaying context above and below it.

### Newer

> **Newer**: `1`

Return messages created after `dateAt`, ordered from oldest to newest. Use when loading newer messages as the user scrolls up from a known position.

### Older

> **Older**: `2`

Return messages created before `dateAt`, ordered from newest to oldest. Use when loading message history as the user scrolls down from a known position.

### Unspecified

> **Unspecified**: `0`

Reserved default value. Do not use in application code.

---

## File: bot-api-reference/enumerations/RootGuidType.md

Enum identifying the entity type encoded in a Root GUID. Used to brand GUID types at compile time and to inspect GUIDs at runtime via `RootGuidUtils.toRootGuidType()`.

## Enumeration Members

### App

> **App**: `9`

An app or bot. Both apps and bots share this type.

### AppOrganization

> **AppOrganization**: `28`

The organization that owns and publishes apps and bots.

### AppVersion

> **AppVersion**: `32`

A published version of an app or bot, including its manifest, permissions, and client assets.

### Asset

> **Asset**: `27`

An uploaded file (image, video, or document). Contains the actual file data and download URLs.

### Badge

> **Badge**: `34`

A profile badge displayed on a user's account (e.g., Beta Tester, Verified Developer).

### Channel

> **Channel**: `4`

A channel within a community.

### ChannelGroup

> **ChannelGroup**: `5`

A channel group within a community.

### CommandIdempotency

> **CommandIdempotency**: `30`

An internal deduplication key used to ensure commands are not executed more than once. Not exposed to developers.

### Community

> **Community**: `2`

A community.

### CommunityApp

> **CommunityApp**: `29`

An app or bot installed in a specific community.

### CommunityLog

> **CommunityLog**: `26`

An entry in a community's audit log.

### CommunityMember

> **CommunityMember**: `31`

A user's membership in a specific community. Distinct from `Person`, which identifies the user account itself. A single user has one `Person` GUID but a separate `CommunityMember` for each community they belong to.

### CommunityMemberBan

> **CommunityMemberBan**: `24`

A ban record preventing a user from accessing a community.

### CommunityMemberInvite

> **CommunityMemberInvite**: `10`

An invitation to join a community.

### CommunityMemberRole

> **CommunityMemberRole**: `21`

A role assignment linking a member to a role within a community.

### CommunityRole

> **CommunityRole**: `11`

A role defined within a community (e.g., Moderator, Admin).

### CommunityRoleMap

> **CommunityRoleMap**: `25`

A permission mapping between a channel or channel group and a role or member. Defines what permissions a role or member has in a specific channel.

### CustomMemberGroup

> **CustomMemberGroup**: `225`

A custom member group defined within a community.

### CustomResource

> **CustomResource**: `224`

Reserved for app developers to create custom entity types within their applications.

### Desktop

> **Desktop**: `18`

A desktop device connected to Root.

### DirectMessage

> **DirectMessage**: `15`

A direct message conversation between users.

### DirectMessageMember

> **DirectMessageMember**: `16`

A participant in a direct message conversation.

### Directory

> **Directory**: `13`

A directory within a channel's file storage.

### Emoji

> **Emoji**: `35`

A custom emoji uploaded to a community.

### Exception

> **Exception**: `20`

A server-side error record. Not exposed to developers.

### File

> **File**: `12`

A file stored in a channel directory.

### Message

> **Message**: `3`

A message in a channel.

### MessageAttachment

> **MessageAttachment**: `14`

A reference linking a message to an asset. Stores metadata like filename and MIME type. The actual file data is stored in the associated `Asset`.

### Mobile

> **Mobile**: `19`

A mobile device connected to Root.

### Person

> **Person**: `1`

A user account. Distinct from `CommunityMember`, which represents a user's membership in a specific community.

### Unknown

> **Unknown**: `0`

The entity type could not be determined.

---

## File: bot-api-reference/enumerations/UserOnlineStatus.md

A community member's current online status.

## Enumeration Members

### Active

> **Active**: `16`

The member is connected and actively using the community.

### Disconnected

> **Disconnected**: `1`

The member is offline (no devices connected to the community).

### Inactive

> **Inactive**: `4`

The member is connected but idle.

### Unspecified

> **Unspecified**: `0`

Status is unknown.

## File: bot-api-reference/type-aliases/AccessRule.md

> **AccessRule** = `object`

Represents a permission override for a specific role or member on a specific channel or channel group.

Access rules enable fine-grained permission control by allowing or denying specific channel permissions for individual roles or members, independent of their default role permissions.

## Properties

### channelOrChannelGroupId

> **channelOrChannelGroupId**: [`ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelorchannelgroupguid)

The `ChannelOrChannelGroupUuid` identifying the channel or channel group this access rule applies to.

### overlay

> **overlay**: [`ChannelOverlayPermission`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeloverlaypermission)

The `ChannelOverlayPermission` object containing the permission overrides. Each permission can be set to `true` (explicitly allow), `false` (explicitly deny), or left undefined (do not modify).

### roleOrMemberId

> **roleOrMemberId**: [`RoleOrMemberGuid`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-roleormemberguid)

The `RoleOrMemberUuid` identifying the role or member this access rule applies to.

---

## File: bot-api-reference/type-aliases/AccessRuleBulkCreateEditDeleteRequest.md

> **AccessRuleBulkCreateEditDeleteRequest** = `object`

## Properties

### creates?

> `optional` **creates**: [`AccessRuleCreateRequest`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrulecreaterequest)[]

### deletes?

> `optional` **deletes**: [`AccessRuleDeleteRequest`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessruledeleterequest)[]

### edits?

> `optional` **edits**: [`AccessRuleEditRequest`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessruleeditrequest)[]

---

## File: bot-api-reference/type-aliases/AccessRuleClient.md

> **AccessRuleClient** = `object`

Service client for managing access rules that control permissions for specific roles or members on channels and channel groups.

Access rules allow fine-grained permission control by overriding the default permissions for specific roles or members on specific channels or channel groups.

Access this client via `rootServer.community.accessRules`.

## Methods

### bulkCreateEditDelete()

> **bulkCreateEditDelete**(`request`: [`AccessRuleBulkCreateEditDeleteRequest`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrulebulkcreateeditdeleterequest), `eventHandlers?`: `object`): `Promise`<`void`>

#### Parameters

| Parameter | Type |
| ------ | ------ |
| `request` | [`AccessRuleBulkCreateEditDeleteRequest`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrulebulkcreateeditdeleterequest) |
| `eventHandlers?` | \{ `channel.created`: [`ChannelCreatedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelcreatedhandler); `channel.deleted`: [`ChannelDeletedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldeletedhandler); `channel.edited`: [`ChannelEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeleditedhandler); `channelGroup.created`: [`ChannelGroupCreatedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupcreatedhandler); `channelGroup.deleted`: [`ChannelGroupDeletedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupdeletedhandler); `channelGroup.edited`: [`ChannelGroupEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupeditedhandler); `community.permission.edited`: [`CommunityPermissionEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitypermissioneditedhandler); \} |
| `eventHandlers.channel.created?` | [`ChannelCreatedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelcreatedhandler) |
| `eventHandlers.channel.deleted?` | [`ChannelDeletedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldeletedhandler) |
| `eventHandlers.channel.edited?` | [`ChannelEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeleditedhandler) |
| `eventHandlers.channelGroup.created?` | [`ChannelGroupCreatedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupcreatedhandler) |
| `eventHandlers.channelGroup.deleted?` | [`ChannelGroupDeletedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupdeletedhandler) |
| `eventHandlers.channelGroup.edited?` | [`ChannelGroupEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupeditedhandler) |
| `eventHandlers.community.permission.edited?` | [`CommunityPermissionEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitypermissioneditedhandler) |

#### Returns

`Promise`<`void`>

#### Example

```ts
import {
  ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid,
  RoleOrMemberGuid,
  AccessRuleBulkCreateEditDeleteRequest,
  rootServer,
} from "@rootsdk/server-bot";

export async function bulkCreateEditDeleteExample(
  channelOrChannelGroupId: ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid,
  roleOrMemberId: RoleOrMemberGuid,
): Promise<void> {
  try {
    // Set up the request
    // 'undefined' values will not modify existing permissions, you can omit them if desired, they're included here for clarity
    const request: AccessRuleBulkCreateEditDeleteRequest = {
      creates: [
        {
          channelOrChannelGroupId: channelOrChannelGroupId,
          roleOrMemberId: roleOrMemberId,
          overlay: {
            channelFullControl: undefined,
            channelView: true,
            channelUseExternalEmoji: undefined,
            channelCreateMessage: true,
            channelDeleteMessageOther: undefined,
            channelManagePinnedMessages: undefined,
            channelViewMessageHistory: undefined,
            channelCreateMessageAttachment: undefined,
            channelCreateMessageMention: undefined,
            channelCreateMessageReaction: undefined,
            channelMakeMessagePublic: undefined,
            channelMoveUserOther: undefined,
            channelVoiceTalk: undefined,
            channelVoiceMuteOther: undefined,
            channelVoiceDeafenOther: undefined,
            channelVoiceKick: undefined,
            channelVideoStreamMedia: undefined,
            channelCreateFile: undefined,
            channelManageFiles: undefined,
            channelViewFile: undefined,
            channelAppKick: undefined,
          },
        },
      ],
      edits: [],
      deletes: [],
    };

    // Call the API
    await rootServer.community.accessRules.bulkCreateEditDelete(request);
  } catch (error) {
    // Detect error
    throw error;
  }
}
```

### create()

> **create**(`request`: [`AccessRuleCreateRequest`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrulecreaterequest), `eventHandlers?`: `object`): `Promise`<`void`>

Creates a new access rule for a role or member on a channel or channel group.

#### Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `request` | [`AccessRuleCreateRequest`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrulecreaterequest) | The access rule configuration including target, subject, and permission overlay. |
| `eventHandlers?` | \{ `channel.created`: [`ChannelCreatedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelcreatedhandler); `channel.deleted`: [`ChannelDeletedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldeletedhandler); `channel.edited`: [`ChannelEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeleditedhandler); `channelGroup.created`: [`ChannelGroupCreatedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupcreatedhandler); `channelGroup.deleted`: [`ChannelGroupDeletedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupdeletedhandler); `channelGroup.edited`: [`ChannelGroupEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupeditedhandler); `community.permission.edited`: [`CommunityPermissionEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitypermissioneditedhandler); \} | Optional handlers for permission update events. Supported event keys: `channel.created`, `channel.edited`, `channel.deleted`, `channelGroup.created`, `channelGroup.edited`, `channelGroup.deleted`, `community.permission.edited`. |
| `eventHandlers.channel.created?` | [`ChannelCreatedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelcreatedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channel.deleted?` | [`ChannelDeletedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldeletedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channel.edited?` | [`ChannelEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeleditedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channelGroup.created?` | [`ChannelGroupCreatedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupcreatedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channelGroup.deleted?` | [`ChannelGroupDeletedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupdeletedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channelGroup.edited?` | [`ChannelGroupEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupeditedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.community.permission.edited?` | [`CommunityPermissionEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitypermissioneditedhandler) | - |

#### Returns

`Promise`<`void`>

A promise that resolves when the access rule is created.

#### Throws

`RootApiException` with `errorCode` set to `NoPermissionToCreate` if missing required permissions, `AlreadyExists` if the rule already exists, or `RequestValidationFailed` if the request is invalid.

#### Example

```ts
import {
  ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid,
  RoleOrMemberGuid,
  AccessRuleCreateRequest,
  rootServer,
} from "@rootsdk/server-bot";

export async function createExample(
  channelOrChannelGroupId: ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid,
  roleOrMemberId: RoleOrMemberGuid,
): Promise<void> {
  try {
    // Set up the request
    // 'undefined' values will not modify existing permissions, you can omit them if desired, they're included here for clarity
    const request: AccessRuleCreateRequest = {
      channelOrChannelGroupId: channelOrChannelGroupId,
      roleOrMemberId: roleOrMemberId,
      overlay: {
        channelFullControl: undefined,
        channelView: true,
        channelUseExternalEmoji: undefined,
        channelCreateMessage: true,
        channelDeleteMessageOther: undefined,
        channelManagePinnedMessages: undefined,
        channelViewMessageHistory: undefined,
        channelCreateMessageAttachment: undefined,
        channelCreateMessageMention: undefined,
        channelCreateMessageReaction: undefined,
        channelMakeMessagePublic: undefined,
        channelMoveUserOther: undefined,
        channelVoiceTalk: undefined,
        channelVoiceMuteOther: undefined,
        channelVoiceDeafenOther: undefined,
        channelVoiceKick: undefined,
        channelVideoStreamMedia: undefined,
        channelCreateFile: undefined,
        channelManageFiles: undefined,
        channelViewFile: undefined,
        channelAppKick: undefined,
      },
    };

    // Call the API
    await rootServer.community.accessRules.create(request);
  } catch (error) {
    // Detect error
    throw error;
  }
}
```

### delete()

> **delete**(`request`: [`AccessRuleDeleteRequest`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessruledeleterequest), `eventHandlers?`: `object`): `Promise`<`void`>

Deletes an access rule.

#### Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `request` | [`AccessRuleDeleteRequest`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessruledeleterequest) | Identifies the access rule to delete. |
| `eventHandlers?` | \{ `channel.created`: [`ChannelCreatedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelcreatedhandler); `channel.deleted`: [`ChannelDeletedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldeletedhandler); `channel.edited`: [`ChannelEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeleditedhandler); `channelGroup.created`: [`ChannelGroupCreatedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupcreatedhandler); `channelGroup.deleted`: [`ChannelGroupDeletedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupdeletedhandler); `channelGroup.edited`: [`ChannelGroupEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupeditedhandler); `community.permission.edited`: [`CommunityPermissionEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitypermissioneditedhandler); \} | Optional handlers for permission update events. Supported event keys: `channel.created`, `channel.edited`, `channel.deleted`, `channelGroup.created`, `channelGroup.edited`, `channelGroup.deleted`, `community.permission.edited`. |
| `eventHandlers.channel.created?` | [`ChannelCreatedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelcreatedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channel.deleted?` | [`ChannelDeletedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeldeletedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channel.edited?` | [`ChannelEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channeleditedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channelGroup.created?` | [`ChannelGroupCreatedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupcreatedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channelGroup.deleted?` | [`ChannelGroupDeletedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupdeletedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channelGroup.edited?` | [`ChannelGroupEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-channelgroupeditedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.community.permission.edited?` | [`CommunityPermissionEditedHandler`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-communitypermissioneditedhandler) | - |

#### Returns

`Promise`<`void`>

A promise that resolves when the deletion completes.

#### Throws

`RootApiException` with `errorCode` set to `NotFound` if the access rule does not exist, or `NoPermissionToDelete` if missing required permissions.

#### Example

```ts
  ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid,
  RoleOrMemberGuid,
  AccessRuleDeleteRequest,
  rootServer,
} from "@rootsdk/server-bot";

  channelOrChannelGroupId: ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid,
  roleOrMemberId: RoleOrMemberGuid,
): Promise<void> {
  try {
    // Set up the request
    const request: AccessRuleDeleteRequest = {
      channelOrChannelGroupId: channelOrChannelGroupId,
      roleOrMemberId: roleOrMemberId,
    };

    // Call the API
    await rootServer.community.accessRules.delete(request);
  } catch (error) {
    // Detect error
    throw error;
  }
}
```

### get()

> **get**(`request`: [`AccessRuleGetRequest`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrulegetrequest)): `Promise`<[`AccessRule`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrule)>

Retrieves a single access rule.

#### Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `request` | [`AccessRuleGetRequest`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrulegetrequest) | Identifies the access rule to retrieve. |

#### Returns

`Promise`<[`AccessRule`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrule)>

A promise that resolves to the `AccessRule` object.

#### Throws

`RootApiException` with `errorCode` set to `NotFound` if the access rule does not exist, or `NoPermissionToRead` if missing required permissions.

#### Example

```ts
import {
  ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid,
  RoleOrMemberGuid,
  AccessRule,
  AccessRuleGetRequest,
  rootServer,
} from "@rootsdk/server-bot";

export async function getExample(
  channelOrChannelGroupId: ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid,
  roleOrMemberId: RoleOrMemberGuid,
): Promise<AccessRule> {
  try {
    // Set up the request
    const request: AccessRuleGetRequest = {
      channelOrChannelGroupId: channelOrChannelGroupId,
      roleOrMemberId: roleOrMemberId,
    };

    // Call the API
    const accessRule: AccessRule =
      await rootServer.community.accessRules.get(request);

    return accessRule;
  } catch (error) {
    // Detect error
    throw error;
  }
}
```

### listByChannelOrChannelGroup()

> **listByChannelOrChannelGroup**(`request`: [`AccessRuleListByChannelOrChannelGroupRequest`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrulelistbychannelorchannelgrouprequest)): `Promise`<[`AccessRule`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrule)[]>

Lists all access rules for a specific channel or channel group.

#### Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `request` | [`AccessRuleListByChannelOrChannelGroupRequest`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrulelistbychannelorchannelgrouprequest) | Identifies the channel or channel group. |

#### Returns

`Promise`<[`AccessRule`](#file-bot-api-reference-type-aliases-accessrule)[]>

A promise that resolves to an array of `AccessRule` objects.

#### Throws

`RootApiException` with `errorCode` set to `NotFound` if the target does not exist, or `NoPermissionToRead` if missing required permissions.

#### Example

```ts
import {
  ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid,
  AccessRule,
  AccessRuleListByChannelOrChannelGroupRequest,
  rootServer,
} from "@rootsdk/server-bot";

export async function listByChannelOrChannelGroupExample(
  channelOrChannelGroupId: ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid,
): Promise<AccessRule[]> {
  try {
    // Set up the request
    const request: AccessRuleListByChannelOrChannelGroupRequest = {
      channelOrChannelGroupId: channelOrChannelGroupId,
    };

    // Call the API
    const accessRules: AccessRule[] =
      await rootServer.community.accessRules.listByChannelOrChannelGroup(
        request,
      );

    return accessRules;
  } catch (error) {
    // Detect error
    throw error;
  }
}
```
